# CLAUDE.md

Guidance for Claude Code when working with this repository.

---

## Project Overview

**JARVIS AI-OS** - An AI-controlled operating system using seL4 microkernel with autonomous decision-making. AI runs at Ring 3 (50-500ms decisions), microkernel runs at Ring 0 (<1ms interrupts). 36-month timeline.

| Phase | Status | Period | Summary |
|-------|--------|--------|---------|
| Phase 0 | COMPLETE | Months 1-6 | Validation (80% success, GO decision) |
| Phase 1 | COMPLETE | Months 6-12 | PoC on x86 QEMU (26/26 weeks, Dec 2025) |
| Phase 2 | COMPLETE | Months 12-24 | Alpha on Pi 4 bare metal (21 drivers, 30-day stability) |
| **Phase 3** | **COMPLETE (beta)** | Months 24-36 | Beta on x86-64 bare metal (**LLM inference on seL4 VERIFIED**) — v0.2.1-beta tagged (30-day soak deferred) |
| Phase 4 | COMPLETE | Months 36+ | Production v1.0 — v1.0.0 SHIPPED 2026-06-26 (tag bdf0951, MIT, public) |
| Phase 5 | SUBSTANTIALLY COMPLETE | Months 36+ | Memory — **all 7 goals mechanism-proven (2026-07-04)**: Arc 1 (#1/#2/#3/#6) deployed default-ON; Arc 2 #5/#4 proven + gated-off (activate Phase 6); #7 folded into #4; `memory` tag proposed as `v1.1.0-memory` (plan: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_PLAN.md`) |
| Phase 6 | IN PROGRESS | Months 36+ | Butler — plan (`phase6/docs/PHASE_6_PLAN.md`) + keystone doc (`PHASE_6_GOAL_K_IT_ACTS.md`, K = "it-acts" spine + live SHIELD). **the COMPLETE Phase-6 flag set co-live**: MEMORY+CONTEXT+RETRIEVAL+CACHE_GROWTH+ACTIONS+MONITORS+WAKE+PROACTIVE) + the live console card rendered from the real feed (Playwright vs the live SSE: total "0", 5 rows "quiet", simulated=false). **The deployed image now runs the FULL proactive butler: memory + the K self-heal action gate + always-on monitors + the event-driven wake lane + the ≥5 registry INFORM behaviors.** Next Phase-6 goals: 6-5 (control-IN) / 6-6 (routing ≥95%) / 6-7 (the 7-day supervised exit).** **Boot_id=17 side-finding 2026-07-11: FIRST on-wire NONZERO v8 validated** — a 28.7 h unattended run (q=28.0M, err=0, restart_count=0) crossed the 1 h + 24 h uptime marks → `monitors_fired=2`/`last_monitor_event=5` on the wire + the two JACT `action=2` uptime records, zero false positives (closes 6-1's one UNVERIFIED item).** **Goal 6-5 (control-IN / natural-language primary — the SECURITY LONG POLE) goal doc authored 2026-07-13 (`phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md`) — PLAN-FIRST, awaiting strategist review; NO code/flag/wire changes.** Turns the read-only console two-way (the box's FIRST untrusted inbound); a 6-item HARD checklist (fuzzed inbound parser + HMAC-SHA256/replay + rate-limit + real QUERY SHIELD closing SEC-039-for-queries + a less-privileged SEC-014 input process + the VIRGIN I211 RX bring-up); host-fuzzable-first ordering (the biggest CI win of the phase) + an M0 RX-feasibility spike that gates the arc + a hard-gated `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` flip ("mostly gated" a NAMED-forbidden failure mode). **Pre-mortem-hardened by a 6-dimension adversarial-review WORKFLOW (24 verified findings, 9 HIGH) — the DIRECTION held but material corrections folded:** the SEC-014 containment was FALSE as first written (BAR0 + IOMMU-off = a bus-master DMA master = root-equivalent → PA keeps BAR0, the process gets RX buffer pages + a doorbell only); a cross-reboot/parser-crash replay hole (→ the sequence floor persists + the replay window is PA-side); the M0 spike would receive NOTHING (the guarded RX init omits `RXDCTL.ENABLE`, the TX-B2 analog); the action keyword-blocklist is a poor query filter (→ a query threat model, not the blocklist); the response LEAKS recalled memory (→ unicast to the console addr only); inbound Q&A is never WRITTEN to episodic (→ the done-when needs the control-IN write); the 6-3 trust seam is LATENT (no v1 TRUST_REQUEST action). 15 open questions for the strategist. Honest ceiling: a bounded, authenticated, rate-limited, SHIELD-scored two-way CONVERSATION — NOT a shell, NOT command execution, NOT the workspace arc; "not yet" is the correct default until "provably safe." **GOAL 6-5 COMPLETE — `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` FLIPPED DEFAULT-ON 2026-07-21 (M4e). The deployed image is now TWO-WAY: the console can hold a bounded, authenticated, replay-protected, rate-limited, SHIELD-scored conversation with the box.** **SCOPE OF "COMPLETE": the SECURITY arc is complete and the channel is live; ONE §14 done-when is NOT met and is carried forward — the canon *multi-turn conversation referencing a fact from a PRIOR control-IN session*. That is a FUNCTIONALITY gap, not a testing gap: `pa_ctrl_gate` deliberately CLEARS the retrieval preamble for a control-IN query (`main_x86.c:2670-2672` — "retrieval-grounded control-IN is a later slice"), so the recall half is UNWIRED (the `EPI_ACT_CONTROL_IN` episodic WRITE half IS proven). Second carry-forward: `main_x86.c:2164` still passes `control_in_available=false` with a now-stale "no control-IN yet" comment — harmless today (no v1 TRUST_REQUEST action) but wrong once one lands.** All 6 checklist items closed, `/security-review` CLEAN, emergency-disable proven. The flip ran TWICE by design: attempt 1 (boot_id=29, real provisioned key) validated the channel and **caught a real bug the earlier campaign structurally could not see** — the input process fed the token bucket a per-frame counter, so it accepted exactly 8 queries per boot then died (`rl=2` with parse/auth/replay all 0). Fails closed, no security hole, but it falsified the standing-channel claim ⇒ flip ABORTED, box reverted, bug fixed first (`bb3ffe9`, PA-stamped `fwd_ms` + a negative-control regression). The re-flip (boot_id=30) passed every leg: **15/15 sustained at human pace** (`acc=55` in one boot vs the old ceiling of 8), a 24-frame non-routing flood **limited at exactly CAP=8 with 16 dropped** then **3/3 recovery**, the browser round-trip coherent + HMAC-verified, `err=0`, audit-clean (49 EXECUTED + 21 BLOCKED, all 25 raw-query probes ZERO). Ubuntu keeps `BootOrder[0]` — control-IN is live only while JARVIS is deliberately booted. Rollback = the retained `=0` image + the JKEY wipe, both proven. **Honest limits carried forward:** the reply is SIGNED, NOT ENCRYPTED; the query SHIELD refuses DEFINED abuse classes and is NOT a general injection detector (containment is structural — K-b + the key never entering Process B); and third-host non-observation of the unicast reply remains **NOT PROVEN** (needs a wired capture point). Next: 6-6 (routing ≥95%) / 6-7 (the 7-day supervised exit). |

Honest limits carried forward: the reply is **SIGNED, NOT ENCRYPTED**; the query SHIELD refuses **DEFINED ABUSE CLASSES** and is NOT a general injection detector (injection is contained STRUCTURALLY — K-b: inbound text can never mint an action); third-host non-observation of the unicast reply is **NOT PROVEN**. **Remaining Phase 6 goals: 6-6 (routing ≥95%) and 6-7 (the 7-day supervised exit)** — 6-5 was the LAST hard-security goal of the phase.

## Architecture

### Model B: Microkernel with AI Control

```
Hardware Layer
    ↓
Microkernel (Ring 0, cores 0-1)
• Interrupt handling (<1ms)
• Memory management, IPC primitives
• ~12K LOC, seL4 (formally verified*)
    ↓ ← Lock-free IPC →
AI Decision Engine (Ring 3, cores 2-N)
• Specialist agents (4-6) — domain-expert agents (device, network, filesystem, user), NOT model-size tiers
• Decision cache (50ms→<1ms for 85% ops)
• SHIELD safety framework
    ↓
User Space Services (Ring 3)
• Device drivers, File systems, Applications
```

> *\*seL4 "(formally verified)" refers to its verified configurations. JARVIS's x86-64 build runs `KernelFastpath=ON`, which is **outside** the verified X64 config ("C-level functional correctness, **no fast path**") — so JARVIS runs a functional-but-unverified seL4 configuration by design (IPC/AVX perf > holding the proof). See `phase4/docs/PHASE_4_GOAL1_INFERENCE_PERF.md` §3.*

**Why:** AI inference (50-500ms) is 3 orders of magnitude too slow for Ring 0 interrupt handling (<1ms). Microkernel isolates time-critical ops from AI.

### Phase 2 Split Deployment (COMPLETE)

```
┌──────────────────┐       UART        ┌──────────────────┐
│   PC (Host)      │◄─────────────────►│   Pi 4 (seL4)    │
│                  │   115200 baud     │                  │
│  Python AI       │                   │  Decision Cache  │
│  - Phi-3 Mini    │   7ms median RTT  │  - 258 patterns  │
│  - Llama 3.2 1B  │                   │  - 85.7% hit     │
│  - SHIELD        │                   │  - <1ms lookup   │
└──────────────────┘                   └──────────────────┘
```

- **Cache hits (85%):** seL4 decision cache answers in <1ms
- **Cache misses (15%):** Forwarded to PC via UART (7ms RTT)
- **No Python on Pi 4:** seL4 userspace is C-only
- Phase 3+ returns to standalone (x86 or Multi-Pi cluster)

### Architecture Enhancements

- **Decision Cache:** Pre-compiled query→bytecode patterns. 135x speedup (50ms→<1ms), 85.7% hit rate.
- **SHIELD Safety:** Staged execution sandbox, continuous risk scoring (0-1.0), shadow execution, deterministic rollback.
- **Shared Context Pool:** Lock-free shared state for agent coordination. 220x faster than serialization.

### Key Constraints

**AI cannot:** handle interrupts (<1ms), generate drivers real-time, run at Ring 0
**AI can:** make high-level decisions (50-500ms), optimize configs, coordinate agents, learn over time

## Coding Conventions & Patterns

### C/Python IPC Struct Alignment

Python ctypes structs MUST exactly match C struct layout:

```python
class RingMessage(ctypes.Structure):
    _pack_ = 1  # REQUIRED: No padding
    _fields_ = [
        ("type", ctypes.c_uint32),
        ("id", ctypes.c_uint32),
        ("payload_size", ctypes.c_uint32),
        ("payload", ctypes.c_char * MAX_MESSAGE_SIZE),
        ("timestamp", ctypes.c_uint64)
    ]
```

### Query Normalization

Both C and Python must normalize identically: lowercase → collapse spaces → trim → FNV-1a 64-bit hash.
- C: `cache_normalize_query()` in `decision_cache.c`
- Python: `QueryProcessor.normalize_query()` in `query_processor.py`

### seL4 Tutorials CMakeLists.txt Pattern

```cmake
include(${SEL4_TUTORIALS_DIR}/settings.cmake)
sel4_tutorials_regenerate_tutorial(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR})
include(rootserver)
include(simulation)
add_executable(hello-world src/main.c ...)
target_link_libraries(hello-world sel4 muslc utils sel4muslcsys sel4platsupport sel4utils sel4debug)
DeclareRootserver(hello-world)
```

Note: `DeclareTutorialApp()` does NOT exist. Use `add_executable()` + `DeclareRootserver()`.

### Testing Philosophy

- Every component has standalone tests printing PASS/FAIL
- C tests: compile and run directly (no deps)
- Python tests: `python test_*.py` (mock mode if seL4 unavailable)
- Always aim for 100% pass rate

### Development Documentation Pattern

- **Phase 2** used weekly status files: `phase2/weeks/weekN/WEEK_N_STATUS.md` (weeks 27-49), committed weekly with the week number in the message.
- **Phase 3** was documented via topic/milestone docs in `phase3/docs/` (KICKOFF, HARDWARE_RESEARCH, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN, the QEMU/native test-results docs, MODEL_BENCH_OFF, the security audits), the ADRs in `docs/decisions/`, and `phase3/docs/PHASE_3_FINAL_REPORT.md` — **not** weekly status files (there is no `phase3/weeks/`).
- **Phase 4** resumes weekly status files at `phase4/weeks/weekN/WEEK_N_STATUS.md` (like Phase 2); Phase 3 used topic docs.
- Implement, test, update the relevant doc; commit with a descriptive subject referencing the milestone/ADR where applicable.

### Bare-Metal Development Rules

- **Always test in QEMU before flashing USB** — run `phase3/scripts/qemu_test.sh` after every build. If it crashes in QEMU, don't waste time reflashing.
- **Build without embedded model for fast iteration** — use `-DJARVIS_EMBED_MODEL=""` for quick boot tests. Model loading is tested separately via the NVMe FAT32 runtime path (the GRUB-module approach was abandoned — seL4 overwrites module memory during rootserver relocation).
- **Verify GRUB menu entry works** — wrong image names cause silent boot failures (keyboard dies, no output). Check grub.cfg image names match `ls ~/sel4-x86/jbuild/images/`.
- **All on-screen UI MUST mirror to the NVMe log (verify UI from the log, not photos)** — the box is headless-except-monitor and showing on-screen UI to a reviewer is impractical and gets worse as the UI grows. So every render routes through a single log-mirror chokepoint (e.g. `fb_status_line` → `[PANEL] <text>`); emit a periodic full-UI snapshot (all fields/widgets in one block) at init + a steady cadence; add a boot-relative timestamp (TSC→ms) so the log shows what **and when**. "UI state fully reconstructable from the log" is an **exit criterion** for every goal-#2+ UI slice. Caveat: the log proves **content, not pixels** — do ONE visual confirm per rendering/font/layout change, then trust the log. Gotcha: anything drawn before `nvme_log_init` is serial-only (re-emit it via the post-init snapshot).
- **UI must track real features (the UI is the living truth-map)** — every user-visible feature/capability MUST be reflected in the UI: primarily the **Remote Telemetry Console** (`phase4/console/`, fed by the `/events` SSE telemetry), secondarily the on-box HUD. When a feature ships, either surface its **real live signal** on the relevant console screen, or expose it in the console's auto-populated **Capabilities/Features** section (driven from real telemetry / a declared feature manifest — never hardcoded claims). Honesty corollary: the UI shows ONLY real, live state — a feature that exists but is missing from the UI is a **gap to close**; anything shown without a live source is **fiction to remove**. So "what's working vs what isn't (or isn't meant to be there)" stays obvious to both the user and the AI at a glance. A new feature means updating the console (or its feature section) **in the same change**; enforced by the console honesty gate (`phase4/console/test_console_honesty.py`).

## Current Status (Phase 3 — Process-Isolated LLM Inference on seL4)

**NVMe RUNTIME MODEL LOADING MILESTONE** (April 4, 2026) — **LLM inference with runtime NVMe model loading on bare-metal seL4 on Ryzen 7 2700X.** 1.6MB USB boot image loads the model GGUF from the NVMe FAT32 partition (Lexar NM790, PCI bus 1) at runtime — model-agnostic, loading whatever `JARVIS_MODEL_FILE` names (current default `"GEMMA2B GUF"` = Gemma 4 E2B Q4_K_M, ~2.89 GiB). NVMe driver: PCI detection → BAR0 MMIO → admin queue → IDENTIFY → I/O queue → FAT32 read → frames → Process B. (Original Apr 4 milestone: 770MB Llama 3.2 1B Q4_K_M → 197K frames.) Coherent text generation via process-isolated IPC. No embedded model binary. Self-test 5/5 PASS, 181 untypeds, 32410MB RAM.

**Phase 3b on real hardware — bare-metal burn-in passed (2026-06-15, NOT a 30-day soak):** clean boot, NVMe model load (LBA-32768), coherent Gemma 4 E2B, heartbeat/shield IPC fix holding at err=0 over 400 queries (q=100/200/300/400), durable NVMe logging with constant boot_id. Formal 30-day x86 soak **DEFERRED — descoped from v0.2.1-beta gating (risk-accepted)**: the new x86 surfaces (shmem_ipc, in-process inference, NVMe load) are covered by this ~400-query burn-in + 300K-iter fuzz + 2 audits + the IPC fix; Phase 2's 30.6-day Pi 4 run (different IPC/ARM stack) is supporting context, not equivalent coverage. See `docs/decisions/2026-06-15-defer-30-day-x86-stability-soak.md`.

## Key Technical Notes

### UART IPC Protocol

```
┌──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬──────────┬──────────┐
│  SYNC    │  TYPE    │   SEQ    │  LENGTH  │  FLAGS   │ PAYLOAD  │  CRC16   │
│ (2 bytes)│ (1 byte) │ (2 bytes)│ (2 bytes)│ (1 byte) │ (0-240)  │ (2 bytes)│
│  0xAA55  │  0x01-0E │  0-65535 │  0-240   │  0x00    │  data    │ CRC-CCITT│
└──────────┴──────────┴──────────┴──────────┴──────────┴──────────┴──────────┘
```

| Type | Value | Direction | Description |
|------|-------|-----------|-------------|
| QUERY | 0x01 | Py→seL4 | Cache lookup |
| RESPONSE | 0x02 | seL4→Py | Cache result |
| HEARTBEAT | 0x03 | Both | Keep-alive |
| HEARTBEAT_ACK | 0x04 | Both | Keep-alive ack |
| STATS_REQUEST | 0x05 | Py→seL4 | Cache stats req |
| STATS_RESPONSE | 0x06 | seL4→Py | Cache stats |
| COMMAND | 0x07 | Py→seL4 | Shell command |
| COMMAND_RESULT | 0x08 | seL4→Py | Command output |
| SHIELD_CHECK | 0x09 | Py→seL4 | Risk assessment |
| SHIELD_RESULT | 0x0A | seL4→Py | Risk decision |
| ERROR | 0x0B | Both | Error |
| RESET | 0x0C | Both | Protocol reset |
| STATE_CHANGE | 0x0D | Py→seL4 | State change |
| STATE_ACK | 0x0E | seL4→Py | State ack |

Baud: 115200. RTT: 7ms measured. Heartbeat: 5s. Timeout: 30s.
Full spec: `phase2/docs/UART_IPC_PROTOCOL.md`

### BCM2711 Hardware

```
GENET Ethernet:  0xFD580000  (separate device untyped!)
Peripheral Base: 0xFE000000
System Timer:    0xFE003000  (1 MHz free-running counter)
GPIO:            0xFE200000
UART0 (PL011):   0xFE201000
EMMC/SDHCI:      0xFE340000
BSC1 I2C:        0xFE804000  (I2C master, 100/400 kHz)
DWC2 USB:        0xFE980000  (USB OTG host controller)
```

USB-Serial wiring: GPIO14(TXD)→RXD, GPIO15(RXD)←TXD, GND─GND

### SD Card Boot (U-Boot)

```
Boot Partition (FAT32):
├── start4.elf      # GPU firmware
├── fixup4.dat      # Memory config
├── u-boot.bin      # U-Boot 2026.01 bootloader
├── boot.scr        # U-Boot boot script
├── kernel8.img     # JARVIS seL4 boot image
├── bcm2711-rpi-4-b.dtb  # Device tree
└── config.txt      # arm_64bit=1, kernel=u-boot.bin, enable_uart=1

Boot flow: GPU firmware → U-Boot → boot.scr → kernel8.img → seL4
```

U-Boot: Press key during 3s countdown for interactive shell. Auto-boot loads kernel8.img at 0x00080000.
Backup: `temp_sd_backup/uboot_working/`

### seL4 Device Mapping Rules

- **Forward-only cursor:** seL4 Untyped_Retype watermark only moves forward. Map devices in ascending paddr order.
- **VSpace range:** vaddr must be within mapped VSpace (0x400000-0x5b9fff). Error 6 = seL4_FailedLookup means vaddr not in VSpace.
- **Init order:** systimer(0xFE003000) → DMA(0xFE007000) → mailbox(0xFE00B000) → watchdog(0xFE100000) → RNG(0xFE104000) → UART/GPIO(0xFE200000-0xFE201000) → SPI(0xFE204000) → PWM(0xFE20C000) → EMMC(0xFE340000) → I2C(0xFE804000) → USB(0xFE980000)
- **Binary buddy skip:** When timer is mapped before UART, use power-of-2 Untyped retypes to advance watermark from 0xFE004000→0xFE200000 (7 retypes: 16KB→32KB→64KB→128KB→256KB→512KB→1MB) instead of 2MB LargePage skip (which would consume GPIO's frame).
- **Device cursor after mapping:** GPIO_BASE + 2*4KB = 0xFE202000
- **DMA = uncacheable:** seL4 does NOT set `SCTLR_EL1.UCI`, so ALL cache maintenance instructions (`DC IVAC/CIVAC/CVAC`) trap from EL0. Map DMA buffers with `vm_attributes = 0` (uncacheable) instead.

### Virtual Address Layout

```
0x5c0000 - UART PL011 (hardcoded)
0x5c1000 - GPIO (hardcoded)
0x5c2000 - System Timer (auto-assigned)
0x5c3000 - EMMC (auto-assigned)
0x5c4000-0x603FFF - DMA pool (256KB)
0x604000-0x609FFF - GENET MMIO (6 pages, own device untyped)
0x610000 - VideoCore Mailbox (explicit vaddr, maps 0xFE00B000)
0x611000 - PM Watchdog (explicit vaddr, maps 0xFE100000)
0x612000 - DMA Engine (explicit vaddr, maps 0xFE007000)
RNG, SPI, PWM - auto-assigned (0xFE104000, 0xFE204000, 0xFE20C000)
0x60A000-0x60CFFF - DWC2 USB (3 pages, paddr 0xFE980000)
```

### Phase 1 IPC Limitation

Phase 1 used "mock IPC" - Python and seL4 did NOT communicate in real-time. Separate memory spaces. Both proven independently, connected in Phase 2 via UART.

### NVMe Write Logging

- Log region: LBA 4000794624 (past p4, the 1.7G vfat tail partition). Drive confirmed via lsblk: **Lexar NM790 2TB**, 4,000,797,360 sectors (1907 GiB). The log's 2701 sectors (header + 2700 entries) fit the ~2,736-sector tail with only ~35 sectors (~18 KB) of headroom — TIGHT. (nvme_log.h's header comments match — "Lexar NM790 2TB", "~2,736-sector tail, ~35 sectors spare".)
- Max entries: 2700 — **circular / rolling buffer** (keeps the latest 2700; never fills): `cursor` is the wrapping write slot (0..2699), `total_entries` is the monotonic lifetime count, and `nvme_log_cursor()` returns entries-stored-capped (rolling-full once total ≥ 2700). `nvme_log_write` no longer returns -2; a stale pre-wrap cursor (== 2700 from the old no-wrap build) is clamped on init. The telemetry `log_cursor` field value/shape is **unchanged** by this (identical until the first wrap) — no wire-contract change.
- Periodic stats cadence: serial `[STATS]` prints every 100 queries (free); the NVMe `LOG_IPC_STATS` entry is written every `JARVIS_STATS_NVME_INTERVAL` = **100** queries (`jarvis_debug.h`). Justified by the **measured** bare-metal rate ~3k queries/day (single core, scalar) → ~870 entries over 30 days, well under the 2700-entry cap. (Task 3 originally used 1000 on a 16k/day estimate the bare-metal run disproved; q=400 err=0 verified on real hardware 2026-06-15. If a future build speeds the query rate up ~5x+, raise this back toward 1000.)
- Format: 512-byte records (header + entries), XOR checksum
- Auto-capture: `puts_serial()` intercept writes every line to NVMe log
- Tags: `[VGA]` = visible on monitor, `[SER]` = serial only, `[PB]` = Process B via MSG_DEBUG IPC
- Recovery: `sudo dd if=/dev/nvme0n1 bs=512 skip=4000794624 count=2701 | python3 phase3/scripts/parse_nvme_log.py` (count=2701 = header + all 2700 slots; the parser reads wrap-order — oldest→newest from `cursor` — once the buffer has rolled)
- `seL4_Yield` only yields to equal-priority — PA and PB both at MaxPrio

### Bare-Metal Debugging Rules

- Use `#if EMMC_DEBUG` preprocessor guards for diagnostics, NOT code deletion (deleting debug code caused mysterious boot failures)
- `seL4_DebugPutChar()` works for TX without device frame mapping
- Device frame mapping required for UART RX
- Serial console: 115200 baud, 8N1
- `put_dec()` and `put_hex()` go through `putc_serial()` -> NVMe log. To suppress NVMe logging temporarily (e.g. verbose dumps), save/clear `g_nvme_ptr`.
- Process A and Process B run at `seL4_MaxPrio` (equal priority). `seL4_Yield` only works between equal-priority threads — MaxPrio-1 gets starved.
- Response ring has 15 slots. PB must reserve 3 for MSG_RESPONSE — use `pb_can_log()` before debug writes.
- `qmodel_forward` stack budget: keep below ~8KB. Large temporaries (>4KB) MUST use `state->fwd_scratch` heap buffer. seL4 Process B has limited stack.
- **PA response consumption: POLL the ring, do NOT `seL4_Wait(resp_notif)` in the workload loop.** The inference path polls and never consumes `resp_notif`, leaving it signaled; heartbeat/shield used to `seL4_Wait(resp_notif)` + a single recv, which returned immediately on that stale signal and read before PB responded (~7% spurious errors, all in hb/shield). Fixed via `wait_for_response()` which polls the ring for the expected type and drains stale messages (race-free, like inference). The only legit `seL4_Wait(resp_notif)` is the pre-loop ready handshake. NOTE: the race is **Heisenbug** — enabling `JARVIS_DBG_IPC` serial prints inserts a scheduling window that masks it, so verify by err-rate ([STATS]) at `JARVIS_DBG_IPC=0`, not per-event logs.

- **Phase 6 Goal 6-5 (Control-IN / Natural-Language Primary) — ✅ COMPLETE 2026-07-21 (`a9c1d9a`): `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` FLIPPED DEFAULT-ON, all 6 checklist items closed, the deployed image is TWO-WAY. **SCOPE OF "COMPLETE": the SECURITY arc is complete and the channel is live; ONE §14 done-when is NOT met and is carried forward — the canon *multi-turn conversation referencing a fact from a PRIOR control-IN session*. That is a FUNCTIONALITY gap, not a testing gap: `pa_ctrl_gate` deliberately CLEARS the retrieval preamble for a control-IN query (`main_x86.c:2670-2672` — "retrieval-grounded control-IN is a later slice"), so the recall half is UNWIRED (the `EPI_ACT_CONTROL_IN` episodic WRITE half IS proven). Second carry-forward: `main_x86.c:2164` still passes `control_in_available=false` with a now-stale "no control-IN yet" comment — harmless today (no v1 TRUST_REQUEST action) but wrong once one lands.** This was the SECURITY LONG POLE and the LAST hard-security goal of the phase; next = 6-6 (routing ≥95%) / 6-7 (the 7-day supervised exit).** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md` — authored 2026-07-13 (post-6-3 next goal — the phase's hardest security gate: it turns the read-only telemetry console two-way and opens the box to the FIRST untrusted inbound it has ever accepted). Canon (`ROADMAP.md:90`): "conversation is the default interface … gated on the full security checklist: auth + HMAC, real SHIELD (close SEC-039), rate-limiting, a hardened/fuzzed inbound parser, and ideally a less-privileged input process (SEC-014)." Honest reading locked: a control-IN message is a QUERY (joins the SAME cache/inference path, returns TEXT; K-b holds — inbound text can never mint an action) — NOT a shell, NOT command execution, NOT the "operates a workspace" arc (Beyond-Phase-7). The 6-item HARD checklist: (1) hardened/fuzzed inbound parser (fragment-reject) · (2) auth + HMAC-SHA256 (net-new bare-metal, constant-time verify) + nonce/sequence replay (floor PERSISTS across reboot) · (3) rate-limit backed by SCHEDULING (input process pinned off the PA core) · (4) real QUERY SHIELD closing SEC-039 for queries (a query threat model — injection/jailbreak — NOT the action keyword blocklist, which false-positives + has zero injection coverage) · (5) a less-privileged SEC-014 input process (a SUBTRACTION from the PB-spawn cap flow — RX buffer pages + a doorbell + one shmem ring, **NOT BAR0**) · (6) the I211 **RX** bring-up (VIRGIN surface — RX code exists but is gated-NULL `main_x86.c:3339`, never run; the guarded init also OMITS `RXDCTL.ENABLE`, the TX-B2 analog). Architecture: I211 RX → the isolated input process → parser → HMAC/replay → rate-limit → a validated query → PA → real SHIELD → cache/inference (retrieval preamble = prior-session recall) → response over the I211 TX to the install-provisioned console address only → two-way console. M0 (the RX-feasibility spike, box — gate the arc) → M1 (the host-fuzzable security core: parser+HMAC+replay+rate-limit + a fuzz harness, host+CI — the BIGGEST CI win of the phase) → M2 (box: the full RX driver + the SEC-014 process) → M3 (query through PA + close SEC-039 + response + two-way UI) → M4 (a `/security-review` pass + a proven emergency-disable) → the hard-gated `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` default-0→ON flip ("mostly gated" is a NAMED, FORBIDDEN failure mode). **Pre-mortem-hardened 2026-07-13 (a 6-dimension adversarial-review WORKFLOW, 24 verified findings folded — the heaviest review of the phase):** the DIRECTION held but 9 HIGH defects were corrected — the SEC-014 containment claim was FALSE as first written (a BAR0-holding parser is a bus-master DMA engine with IOMMU-off = root-equivalent → PA keeps BAR0, the process gets RX pages + a doorbell); a cross-reboot / parser-crash replay hole (persistent key + reset-to-0 window → the sequence floor persists + the window is PA-side); the M0 spike would receive NOTHING (missing `RXDCTL.ENABLE`); the action keyword-blocklist is a poor query filter (→ a query threat model); the response LEAKS recalled memory (→ unicast to the console addr only); inbound Q&A is never WRITTEN to episodic (→ the done-when needs the control-IN write, not a synthetic fact); the 6-3 trust seam is LATENT (no v1 TRUST_REQUEST action); + sender-side key provisioning. 15 open questions (O-Q1–O-Q15) for the strategist. Honest ceiling: a bounded, authenticated, rate-limited, SHIELD-scored two-way CONVERSATION on a small model — interactivity behind a hard security gate; "not yet" is the correct default until "provably safe." **FLIP EVIDENCE (M4e, 2026-07-21, boot_id=30, supervised bare metal):** deployed image md5 `ba93fe4ecf8b098e1ccbc513bdbb6d76` verified byte-identical to a rebuild from committed source; V-LOAD **15/15** sustained at human pace (`acc=55` in one boot vs the old 8-frame ceiling); a 24-frame non-routing flood limited at exactly **CAP=8 with 16 dropped**, then **3/3 recovery**; durable `[CTRL-IN-STATS] acc=55 drop=16 (parse=0 rl=16 auth=0 replay=0) bp=0 down=0`; JACT **49 EXECUTED + 21 BLOCKED** with all **25 raw-query probes ZERO** (a refusal records the reason-class LABEL only); `err=0` at `q=175,600`. Rollback = the retained `sel4test-driver-image-x86_64-pc99.bak-pre65flip` md5 `379f6bdb2acfa0c685a710f794723bad` (on the ESP and `~/jarvis_63_image.bak-pre65flip`) and/or dd-zeroing the JKEY slot — both proven; Ubuntu keeps `BootOrder[0]`.
- **Phase 6 Goal 6-5 FINAL REPORT:** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_FINAL_REPORT.md` — the standalone 6-5 final report (what shipped per checklist item, the module/CI inventory, the flip + validation evidence, and the honest limitations carried forward).
- **Fault-EP Validity Predicate (Phase 6 K/M4 pre-flip, host):** `phase3/src/ai/km2b_fault.c/h` — the `pa_poll_fault` label-gate **defense-in-depth** hardening ahead of the deploy-live `JARVIS_ACTIONS` flip (§10 carry-forward #2). `km2b_fault_is_genuine(label,badge,ip,n_workers,text_lo,text_hi)` — a fault-EP `seL4_NBRecv` result is GENUINE (not an empty-EP phantom) iff **label ∈ 1..15 AND badge ∈ 0..`g_pb_workers_started`**; the badge is a SEPARATE stale-register class the STEP-3 label-only gate was blind to, so requiring BOTH is multiplicatively less likely to coincide. `km2b_fault_ip_plausible` is **ADVISORY only** (logs `[FAULT-IP-WARN]`, NEVER a veto — a genuine jump-to-garbage VMFault legitimately faults outside `.text` yet MUST still restart); it returns 0 only for a VMFault (label 5) whose ip is outside PB's `.text` window `[lo,hi)`, and degrades to always-plausible when the window is unknown. The window is stashed once at spawn (`stash_pb_text_window`, the PF_X PT_LOAD union; ET_EXEC ⇒ vaddr==runtime addr). Wired into `pa_poll_fault` (all `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS`) after the label gate + before the `[FAULT]` print — a rejected phantom logs `[FAULT-REJECT]` + `break`s, never reaching `pa_restart_pb`; a genuine fault is NEVER rejected (host X3 + the box gate: 0 false `[FAULT-REJECT]` across the induced-fault probes). `test_km2b_fault.c` **21/21** (incl. the teeth-proven X3 advisory-≠-verdict guard — a mutant folding the advisory into the verdict fails X3 alone) → CI "Phase 6: K/M4 fault validity predicate (C)". Host-pure (no seL4 dep, mirrors `km2b_miss.h`); OFF (`JARVIS_ACTIONS=0`) byte-identical by construction (whole wiring + the stash gated). Called only under `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS` (default 0).
- **Hang/Wedge Miss-Counter (Phase 6 K/M2c, host):** `phase3/src/ai/km2b_miss.c/h` — the shared consecutive-PB-contact-miss state machine that catches PB **alive-but-WEDGED** (no fault fires, the fault EP stays silent — the failure the STEP-3 crash lane can't see). `km2b_miss_on_pb_timeout(lane)` advances, `km2b_miss_on_pb_ack` resets to 0 (on a GENUINE typed dequeue only, never a bare `seL4_Wait`), `km2b_miss_tripped(threshold)` = `count >= threshold`. The counter moves ONLY on explicit calls — no "absence looks like an event" (the STEP-3 phantom class). Wired at the 3 PB-contact timeout/success sites in `main_x86.c` (all `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS`); the cache lane + `cg_served_now` call NEITHER (D4 neutrality); the single `next_query` trigger funnels `pa_restart_pb("hang", …)` at threshold 3. `test_km2b_miss.c` **22/22** (+T11 NULL-arg / T12 threshold-0) → CI "Phase 6: K/M2c hang miss-counter (C)". Box-proven: 5/5 hang cycles `miss_count=3 fault_EP=silent -> TRIPPED -> coherent recovery`; the JACT trail (`respawn hang lane=2 miss=3`) reads back off-box. Detects deschedule-able wedges (blocked-on-IPC / yield-spin). A hard same-core busy-loop is **CONFIRMED NOT detectable** (K/M4 box experiment 2026-07-07, **Outcome B**): a non-yielding PB-main `for(;;){}` STARVES PA on the shared core 0 (PA's production yield-poll hands the core to PB), so PA emits 0 detection lines while a soft-yield hang was detected 5/5 in the same run — only deschedule-able wedges + crashes (fault deschedules PB) are caught. Mitigation = a dedicated PA core (K/M4 carry-forward #3), or accept the documented limitation (hard non-yielding loop → power-cycle, a narrow class). **D1/D2 follow-up (next commit):** the inference dispatch guard decoupled from `#if JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH`; the hang-trip polls the fault EP first so a crash-during-HB/shield-wait audits as "fault" not "hang".
- **Control-IN Security Core (Phase 6 6-5/M1, host-pure — NOW LIVE IN THE DEPLOYED IMAGE since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21; the "DEPLOY-INERT" status below is M1-era history):** `phase3/src/crypto/` (`sha256.c/h` = copied public-domain Brad-Conte SHA-256, 3 NIST KATs; `hmac_sha256.c/h` = RFC 2104 + a **constant-time** `hmac_sha256_verify` — `volatile` OR-accumulator over all 32 tag bytes, NO early-out, deliberately NOT the `shmem_ipc.c` `!=` compare; `{sha256,hmac}_vectors.h` = committed differential-fuzz vectors) + `phase3/src/net/` (`control_msg.h` = the authoritative 36-B-header/≤240-B wire format, big-endian, **canonical HMAC span = payload[0..len−32)**; `control_parser.c/h` = hardened copy-free Eth/IPv4/UDP+JCTL parse — **fragment-reject (MF|offset) before any UDP/HMAC work**, exact length math, `IP_MIN_HDR`=20 guard; `control_replay.c/h` = PA-side seq-floor + epoch + 128-nonce ring, ordered epoch→seq→nonce truth table, persistence contract; `control_ratelimit.c/h` = uint32-wrap-safe milli-token bucket (millisecond-driven by the CALLER — feeding it a per-frame counter starves the channel at exactly CAP; see the M4e finding); `control_verify.c/h` = the orchestrator, order parse→ratelimit→HMAC→replay so **`control_replay_check` MUTATES state ONLY after a valid HMAC** — an unauth frame can never walk the floor / fill the ring; `fuzz_control_in.c` = 300K-iter ASan/UBSan) + `phase3/scripts/gen_control_vectors.py`. Built by a 5-phase Workflow (crypto leaves verified vs NIST/RFC vectors BEFORE dependents) + an adversarial-review agent that caught a **`len==14` 1-byte parser OOB read** (`ip[0]` with only `len≥14`) → fixed + the fuzz hardened to **exact-len heap buffers** (ASan now redzones any read-past-`len`; teeth-proven vs a guard-removed mutant → `heap-buffer-overflow READ`). **Per-module CI counts (re-measured 2026-07-21, all green):** SHA-256 = 3 NIST KATs · HMAC-SHA256 = RFC 4231 cases 1/2/4/6 + the constant-time verify sweep · `control_parser` **43** · `control_replay` **23** · `control_ratelimit` **13** (was 9; +4 at the M4e clock fix) · `control_verify` **22** · `fuzz_control_in` **300,020** iters ASan/UBSan. **M1-era status (history): deploy-inert — nothing linked into the box.** M2a wired it into the build behind `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`; the SEC-014 less-privileged input process is M2b; the query SHIELD (SEC-039-for-queries) is M3. **CURRENT: all of this is LIVE in the deployed image** (`JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` default-ON since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21) — every inbound frame is parsed, rate-limited, HMAC-verified and replay-checked by this code on real hardware. Doc: `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md` §9.
- **Control-IN RX Data Path (Phase 6 6-5/M2a, box — `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` is DEFAULT-ON since 2026-07-21, so this RX path is LIVE; "default-0 / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M2a-era history):** the RX-productization half of the goal-doc M2 — real I211 RX ring → the M1 `control_verify` (running in PA) → LOG the validated query ONLY (the **M3 BOUNDARY**: no routing to inference; the route + query SHIELD is M3). `nic_i211.c` gains gated step-6 RX programming (SRRCTL-legacy + `RXDCTL.ENABLE` the M0 silent-RX fix + RDT-armed-last; `#else` = verbatim HEAD `RDT=255` for obj-identity — nic_i211.c doesn't include jarvis_debug.h so the gate rides a build-script `-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1`). `main_x86.c` (all `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): the [NET] block allocs the RX ring + 256 buffers and arms RDT only if all 256 wired → `g_net.nic`; boot reads the HMAC key FAIL-CLOSED from the NVMe JKEY slot (`control_key.h` @ LBA 21,130,000; key lives in PA, never the future input process, never BAR0) + inits `control_replay`/`control_ratelimit` (fixed `CONTROL_TEST_EPOCH`, per-boot floor 0 — M3 = real epoch + persisted floor); the workload-loop poll (`i211_nic_recv`→`control_verify`) LOGs a sanitized ACCEPT (durable, bounded to 8) / silent DROP counters; `[CTRL-IN-STATS]` durable at the [STATS] cadence. `control_verify` gains additive `seq`/`boot_epoch` (set on CV_ACCEPT only; M1 CI still green). `build_jarvis_x86.sh` GATES the crypto/net source copy + CMake inject + the `-D` + include dirs on the header flag being 1, with a **symmetric teardown `else`** (an adversarial-review HIGH: the persistent `-D` overrode the header's `#ifndef 0` → a silent "OFF-stays-ON" after an ON rebuild; the teardown strips the injected state). **Full box gate PASSED 2026-07-15:** OFF-identity (main.c.obj+nic_i211.c.obj byte-identical, proven 3× incl. post-teardown) + KVM probe (`JARVIS_CONTROL_IN_PROBE`: key-loaded → synthetic frame ACCEPT + tamper DROP_AUTH) + **bare-metal signed frames** (boot 22 unicast + boot 23 broadcast, both accepted: `[CTRL-IN] ACCEPT seq=1/2/3` + `[CTRL-IN-STATS] acc=3 (auth=1 replay=1)`, err=0, LAN/telemetry broadcast parse-dropped on wrong port). A host cross-check (scapy frame → C `control_verify` → ACCEPT/DROP_AUTH) proved the wire format before the box. Doc §9; flags `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`/`_PROBE`.
- **Control-IN Input Process (Phase 6 6-5/M2b-1, box — LIVE since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21; `phase3/src/sel4/input_server.c`, 108 LOC. "default-0 / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M2b-1-era history):** the SEC-014 **isolation split** (Model 2, goal-doc §5) — the untrusted PARSE moves OFF Process A into a NEW least-privileged third seL4 process `jarvis-input` (a sibling CPIO app to `jarvis-inference`). `phase3/src/sel4/input_server.c` (the process main: `seL4_Wait(wake)` → read the PA→input RAW mailbox → `control_parse_frame`/`control_parse_msg` + `control_ratelimit` ONLY → publish the isolated JCTL bytes + a status hint to the input→PA CAND mailbox → `seL4_Signal(ack)`) + `phase3/src/net/control_mailbox.h` (the two 4 KB release/acquire mailboxes, x86-TSO handshake, single-frame-in-flight backpressure). **The input process holds NO timer caps and has no clock, so PA STAMPS `ctrl_raw_mbx_t.fwd_ms` (`jarvis_uptime_ms`) into the raw mailbox alongside the payload — published by the same RELEASE store — and the input process feeds THAT to `control_ratelimit`** (M4e pre-flip fix; M2b-1 shipped a per-frame `tick++` placeholder that refilled 1 milli-token per frame against a 1000 milli-token cost, so the channel accepted exactly 8 frames and then died — bare-metal-confirmed `[CTRL-IN-STATS] acc=8 drop=2 (… rl=2 …)`. Fails CLOSED, no security hole, but it made the standing-conversation claim false). The input process holds **ZERO NIC caps, no HMAC key, no request/response ring, no model caps** — a cap-subtraction from `spawn_inference_process` (it gets only its TCB/CSpace/VSpace/IPC buffer + the 2 mailbox frames + 2 notifications + CPU, `seL4_MaxPrio-1` pinned off PA's core). PA keeps BAR0 + polls the NIC + copies each raw frame into the RAW mailbox; `main_x86.c` (all `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): `spawn_input_process` (config + map/dup the 2 mailbox frames into PA + input + copy the 2 notif caps + argv the child cslots), `pa_verify_candidate` (PA **RE-PARSES** the candidate ITSELF then HMAC + replay — verify-in-PA, O-Q3c, never trusts a forwarded offset), and the live-poll rewrite (consume-candidate then forward-one-frame); the rate limiter moved off PA INTO the input process. `build_jarvis_x86.sh` creates the `jarvis-input` app + registers it (`add_subdirectory(../jarvis-input)` + extends `MakeCPIO`) under the gate, with a symmetric teardown `else` so an OFF build packs no input ELF. **Box gate PASSED 2026-07-15:** OFF object-identity (`main.c.obj` .text/.rodata/.data + `nm` byte-identical to the pre-M2b-1 baseline; no `jarvis-input` ELF / `crypto`/`net` objects when off) + KVM split-pipeline PROBE (`-smp 6`, CONTROL_IN=1/PROBE=1): `[CTRL-IN-PROBE] accept q=status cand_seq=1` (the round trip PA→input→PA — `cand_seq` PROVES the input process produced the candidate, not a PA-only shortcut) + `tamper=DROP_AUTH`, `[CTRL-IN] input process spawned (SEC-014, parse+ratelimit only)`, 5 workers/NN=6, coherent Gemma, err=0, 0 faults/restarts. No telemetry/console change (on-wire control-IN telemetry is M3). M2b-2 = bare-metal + flood-doesn't-starve-PA + liveness-heartbeat + drop-`RCTL.BAM` hardening. Doc §9; flags `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`/`_PROBE`.
- **Control-IN Input-Process Hardening (Phase 6 6-5/M2b-2, box — LIVE since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21; "default-0 / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M2b-2-era history):** the SEC-014 **liveness + degrade + flood/SEC-033 + drop-BAM** hardening that **CLOSES goal-doc item-5** (DONE 2026-07-16: KVM-proven + the supervised bare-metal WIRE PROOF, boot_id=24 — `acc=3` / `DROP_REPLAY` / `DROP_AUTH`, the flood rate-limited `rl`→488 with err=0 to q=13,700 / 0 faults / `bp=0` / `down=0`, and `parse=0` = all broadcast hardware-filtered by the dropped BAM; box reverted clean to the 6-3 image). **Liveness:** a new `km2b_miss` DEADLINE-WINDOW lane `KM2B_LANE_INPUT=5` (`km2b_miss.h`) — a wedged input sticks `g_ctrl_inflight=1` so PA stops forwarding (a frame-counted miss would never trip), so the miss advances once per `CTRL_IN_DEADLINE_ITERS`=32 PA-active iterations one in-flight frame stays unanswered (q_total-keyed → the deadline FREEZES during an inference, so a live input starved by worker-5 never false-trips; idle-safe). At `CTRL_IN_MISS_THRESHOLD`=3 → latch `g_ctrl_in_down` + `mon_notify(MON_EV_INPUT_DEAD)` → `[ANOMALY] mon input-dead` + JACT `action=2` + `monitors_fired++` through the K spine (zero new plumbing). DETECT + DEGRADE only (a jarvis-input respawn is M3 — the leaky allocator forbids naive respawn); a dead input = control-IN unavailable until reboot. **`main_x86.c`** (all `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): the `ctrl_in_liveness_tick(now_iters)` shared helper (poll site passes `q_total`, PROBE passes synthetic counts), the poll-site outer `!g_ctrl_in_down` gate + `km2b_miss_on_pb_ack` on candidate-consume + the deadline tick + the STAMP on forward + the backpressure drain-into-`bp_scratch`+`g_ctrl_bp_drops++` else-branch, the `[CTRL-IN-STATS]` `bp=`/`down=` fields, and PROBE mode 2 (`seL4_TCB_Suspend` the input TCB → 3 ticks → degrade; INSIDE the `g_input_ready` guard). **`monitors.h`/`monitors.c`** (`#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): `MON_EV_INPUT_DEAD` + its keyword-clean `"mon input-dead lane=N miss=N"` snapshot case (T7-pinned via a new `-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1` CI compile). **`nic_i211.c`:** RCTL drops `BAM` (unicast-only RX; RA[0]/AV exact-MAC filter is BAM-independent; TX telemetry unaffected) under `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN && !defined(JARVIS_CONTROL_IN_BAM)`; the `#else` = verbatim HEAD (`nic_i211.c.obj` OFF-identical). **`jarvis_debug.h`:** PROBE-mode doc + `#error` cross-guards (CONTROL_IN_PROBE vs other `*_PROBE`; PROBE==2 requires MONITORS && ACTIONS) + the `CONTROL_IN_BAM` escape-hatch note. Pre-mortem-hardened (a 6-lens adversarial-review WORKFLOW — 33 findings, 9 folded) + a 3-lens diff-review (clean). Box gate: **OFF 4-object identity** (`main.c.obj`+`nic_i211.c.obj`+`monitors.c.obj`+`km2b_miss.c.obj` .text/.rodata/.data + `nm` byte-identical) + host green (`test_monitors` host-pure **and** `-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1` both 44 PASS, `test_km2b_miss` 22 PASS) + KVM induced-death PROBE. Option-A scheduling confirmed (input MaxPrio-1 on core `g_num_nodes-1`; Option B = a permanent ~15–20% Gemma hit, fallback only). Doc §9; flags `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`/`_PROBE`(=2 induced-death)/`_BAM`.
- **Control-IN Query SHIELD (Phase 6 6-5/M3-1, host-pure — NOW LIVE on the deployed box since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21, scoring every inbound query before PA routes it; "HOST + CI ONLY / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M3-1-era history):** the query-threat gate that closes **SEC-039-for-queries at the LOGIC level** — a control-IN frame that survived M1/M2b auth+replay yields a ≤172 B validated QUERY, and `query_shield.c/h` scores it QS_ALLOW vs QS_REFUSE for four DEFINED abuse classes (KEY_EXTRACTION / EXFIL / JAILBREAK / CONFIG_DISCLOSE) BEFORE PA would route it (the box route + induced-BLOCK proof + response are M3-2). **THE HONESTY CEILING (O-Q4, the crux):** a COARSE abuse-refuser for tractable classes, NOT an injection detector — a local Gemma + no injection detector can't catch novel/obfuscated prompt injection; injection is contained STRUCTURALLY (K-b: inbound text can never mint an action; tagged-untrusted → no cache-growth/retrieval store contamination; answer-to-console-address-only → no exfil). The ONLY honest claim: *"refuses a defined abuse class + audits it; general injection contained structurally — not detected."* BANNED anywhere: "blocks injection"/"detects malicious intent"/"prevents prompt injection"/"100% blocked". Engine: `qs_normalize` (length-carried, lowercase alnum words, every non-alnum byte → a single-space separator; NEVER strlen'd) → a static human-reviewed table of EMIT-ANCHORED ORDERED phrase patterns — an emission verb (a 23-verb set) + the possessive `your`/a sensitive target within a TIGHT per-pattern gap (up to 4 slots; the canned jailbreak requires adjacency gap-1; first-match-wins, severity-ordered so a combined attack resolves to the most severe class). Audit discipline (`query_shield_reason_label`): a REFUSE records the reason-class LABEL ONLY, never the raw query — so a hostile can't smuggle a blocklist keyword (e.g. "format") into the M3-2 JACT audit; labels are keyword-clean by construction, teeth-proven BOTH ways (label → `shield_assess` EXECUTE + a bounded substring proving the raw query is never inside the label). Corpora: `hostile_queries.h` (20, 1:1 to the patterns + 2 severity-ordered combined) + `benign_queries.h` (100 realistic status/design/dev/count queries — the FP denominator). `test_query_shield.c` (**87 asserts, measured FP = 0/100** printed + hard-fail-if-nonzero — the phase-exit number) + `fuzz_query_shield.c` (300K ASan/UBSan: RAW exact-len-heap + match-preserving-mutation STRUCTURED modes). **Design pre-mortem-hardened by 3 adversarial-review WORKFLOWS** (opus/high; ~33 realistic FPs folded → the systemic bare-possessive→emit-anchor retune; dropped the FP-prone `{key,slot}`/`{action,ids}`/DAN/`{dump,episodic}` rows; the design-opinion gap-4 tighten to gap-2; the 4-slot `{EMIT,your,system,prompt}` for the prompt word-sense). CI: 2 steps (unit links `shield_action.c`+`action_allowlist.c`+`shield_learn.c` for the keyword-clean teeth, `test_monitors` precedent; fuzz is `query_shield.c` only; unit **87 asserts**, fuzz **300,000** iters ASan/UBSan). **M3-1-era status (history): deploy-inert.** **CURRENT: LIVE** — since the 6-5 flip every validated inbound query is scored here before PA will route it, and a refusal is audited by reason-class LABEL only. v11 `control_in_blocked` = a **defined-abuse-class refuse count**, NOT "injection blocked" (the honesty-gate contract); general injection remains contained STRUCTURALLY, not detected. Doc: `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md` (M3-1).
- **Control-IN MODULE INDEX (Phase 6 6-5 — the complete file map; the narrative entries above/below carry the design rationale).** **41 files / 6,733 LOC** (measured 2026-07-21 via `git ls-files` + `wc -l`), **13 CI steps** all green. All of it is LIVE in the deployed image since the flip.
  - `phase3/src/crypto/` (8 files, 1,554 LOC) — `sha256.c/h` (**3 NIST KATs**) · `hmac_sha256.c/h` (RFC 4231 cases 1/2/4/6 + the **constant-time** `hmac_sha256_verify` sweep) · `sha256_vectors.h` + `hmac_vectors.h` (committed differential vectors) · `test_sha256.c` + `test_hmac_sha256.c`.
  - `phase3/src/net/` (26 files, 4,061 LOC) — `control_msg.h` (the authoritative JCTL wire format: 36 B big-endian header, ≤240 B, HMAC span `payload[0..len−32)`) · `control_key.h` (the JKEY slot @ LBA 21,130,000, WRITE-ONCE, read fail-closed) · `control_mailbox.h` (the two 4 KB release/acquire PA↔input mailboxes; carries PA's `fwd_ms` clock stamp — the input process has no timer cap) · `control_parser.c/h` (**43**) · `control_replay.c/h` (**23**) · `control_ratelimit.c/h` (**13**) · `control_verify.c/h` (**22**) · `control_floor.c/h` (**48**) · `control_console.c/h` (**70**) · `control_reply.c/h` (**86**) · `test_control_signer_differential.c` (**47** — pins the Python signer against the box's own `control_verify`) · `fuzz_control_in.c` (**300,020** iters ASan/UBSan) + the 8 `test_control_*.c`.
  - `phase3/src/ai/` query-SHIELD group (6 files, 1,010 LOC) — `query_shield.c/h` (**87** asserts, **measured FP = 0/100**) · `hostile_queries.h` (20) + `benign_queries.h` (100, the FP denominator) · `test_query_shield.c` · `fuzz_query_shield.c` (**300,000** iters ASan/UBSan).
  - `phase3/src/sel4/input_server.c` (108 LOC) — the SEC-014 least-privileged `jarvis-input` process: parse + rate-limit ONLY, **zero NIC caps, no HMAC key, no request/response ring, no model caps**.
  - `phase3/src/drivers/net_udp.c/h` — `net_build_udp_unicast` (the gated unicast reply builder; fail-closed on a NULL dst MAC, NEVER falls back to broadcast). `test_net_udp.c` **24** (broadcast build) / **42** (`-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1`).
  - `phase3/src/drivers/nvme.c/h` — `nvme_flush` (NVM FLUSH opcode 0x00, control-IN-gated) makes the replay-floor write NAND-durable across cold power loss.

- **Cache Growth (Phase 5 #6/M0+M1):** `phase3/src/ai/cache_growth.c/h` — pure-C promotion **selector** (`cg_select_promotions`: counts `query_key` frequency over a scanned episodic window, promotes keys with frequency ≥ `CG_PROMOTE_THRESHOLD`=2, emits the **newest** candidate per key, dedups by key; borrowed text carried by pointer + `*_len`, never `strlen`'d — no seL4/device/`decision_cache` dep) + **(M1) the Option-B rolling frequency aggregate** (`cg_freq_slot_t[CG_FREQ_CAP=1024]` open-addressed linear-probe table, `count==0`=empty sentinel; `cg_freq_bump` inserts-or-increments and returns the new count (0 if full+absent), `cg_freq_get` probes-to-empty — pure, closes the SYSTEM_DESIGN "frequency-signal gap": the M5 recall index is deduped-to-newest and can't count). Frequency-based + deterministic — the cache **learns frequently-asked queries and serves them fast**, never "understands". Host-tested `test_cache_growth.c` (**22 PASS**: T1–T5 threshold/dedup/newest-wins/n=0/max-cap + M1 T6–T9 bump/get, independent counts, collision probing, saturation) → CI step "Phase 5: Cache-Growth promotion logic (C)". **The dormant SEC-024 LRU eviction goes LIVE + tested** (`decision_cache.c:214-238`, the phase3 copy's fill-then-evict path, unreached at ~308/512): `test_decision_cache_lru.c` (**10/10**: fill to `CACHE_SIZE`=512 → access-control the LRU → insert a 513th key → oldest-accessed evicted, `entries_used` stays 512, new key present, recently-accessed survivors kept) → CI step "Phase 5: Decision-cache LRU eviction (C)". **M1 (box wiring, 2026-07-03):** Process A (`main_x86.c`, all `#if JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH`) seeds the aggregate at the boot recall-scan (outer gate now `(JARVIS_G3_RETRIEVAL || JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH)`), snapshots `g_cache_baseline` after pattern load, and at the `[STATS]` cadence (BEFORE `epi_commit` clears the batch) folds the batch then promotes usable INFER answers (`EPI_ACT_INFER`+`EPI_OUT_OK`+`resp_len>0`, freq≥2, newest-per-key, normalize-or-skip, resp clamped to `MAX_ACTION_LEN-1`) via `cache_insert` (update-in-place = idempotent; a promotion counts only when `entries_used` grows) under `CG_PROMOTE_HWM` (409 = 0.8×512, §7 — EMPTY slots survive; SEC-024 LRU stays a guardrail); `[CACHE-GROW] promoted=/used=/grow=/hwm=` proof line; `build_jarvis_x86.sh` syncs + injects `cache_growth.c` into the PA source list. Gated `JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH` default-0 (transient sed flip for the M1 smoke; default flips at M3). Design: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL6_CACHE_GROWTH.md`.

Keys = episodic `query_key` (FNV-1a parity — the deliberate mapping change from Phase-1's action_type). **MONITOR-ONLY (D-c): learns + surfaces, NEVER blocks — SEC-039 unchanged; enforcement is Phase 6.** Persistence = D-a derive-from-episodic (ERROR/BLOCKED records; boot-scan seed + batch fold at M1 — no second NVMe store). Design: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL5_SHIELD_LEARNING.md`.
- **Semantic Memory (Phase 5 #4/M0):** `phase3/src/ai/semantic_store.c/h` — a SEPARATE raw-LBA circular **fact store** (the `episodic_store` clone: header + 512 B records, XOR checksum, boot_id, circular cursor + monotonic total, flush-after-write, device-independent callbacks) at `SEM_STORE_BASE_LBA` **21,110,000** (+4097 sectors ≈ 2 MiB, clear of episodic which ends @ 21,108,192; RESERVED like the episodic region), magic "JSEM", `semantic_fact_t` = `{boot_id, seq, t_ms, key, fact_type, support_count, confidence_x100, text_len, text[440]}` (exactly 512 B) — **plus `sem_store_upsert`**: insert-or-raise-support (a repeated subject UPDATES its fact — text/t_ms follow the newest, `support_count = max(existing, incoming)` = monotonic + **idempotent across boot re-distills**, seq keeps identity; never a dup). `phase3/src/ai/semantic_distill.c/h` — the pure **DETERMINISTIC distill** (`sd_distill`, **#7 consolidation's compact-core, landed here**): group `epi_record_t[]` by `query_key`, support = usable records only (`sd_record_usable` = INFER+OK+resp>0, the #6/G3 filter — cache echoes/failures never become "facts"), emit the NEWEST usable answer per key at `support >= SEM_MIN_SUPPORT` (=3); `confidence_x100` = % of usable same-key answers byte-identical to the chosen one; text copied by `resp_len` never strlen. **NO LLM, NO embeddings (PLAN §7.2/§7.6 — Phase 7); honest ceiling = "compacts what it has repeatedly seen" (observable patterns), NEVER "knows preferences"/"understands".** #4 = store + distill only; the G3 retrieval hook is a future slice; **#4/#7 are completeness/Phase-6-readiness goals — Phase 5's canonical done-when is already met.** Host-tested `test_semantic_store.c` (**6 PASS**: fresh-init/layout, round-trip + stamps, reboot-bump persist, corrupt-header, wrap, upsert-monotonic) + `test_semantic_distill.c` (**8 PASS**: threshold, support + FNV-1a key parity, newest-wins + honest confidence, dedup, guards, max-cap, usable filter, length-not-strlen) → CI steps "Phase 5: Semantic store (C)" + "Phase 5: Semantic distill (C)". **M1 (box wiring, 2026-07-04, BOX-VERIFIED):** gated `JARVIS_SEMANTIC` (jarvis_debug.h, **default 0**) in Process A — `sem_store_init(&g_semantic, epi_nvme_read/write, …)` right after episodic-ready; the boot recall-scan buffers the newest ≤`SEM_DISTILL_WINDOW`(1024) records tail-only/chronological (no ring — `sd_distill`'s newest-wins is position-based) then runs the one-shot distill + upserts with a `[SEM] window=/facts=/upserted=/stored=` proof line; `build_jarvis_x86.sh` syncs + injects both .c's into the PA source list. **WRITE-ONLY at M1** — the store is never read in inference/routing. S0-snapshot OFF-vs-ON smoke: ON `[SEM] window=1024 facts=1 upserted=1 stored=1`, OFF 0×`[SEM]`, `[INFER]` byte-identical (16=16), err=0. Design: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL4_SEMANTIC_MEMORY.md`.
